===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.available_stock = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
            return False

        self.users[user_id] = {
            'cart': {},
            'details': {}
        }
        self.reservations[user_id] = {}
        return True

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
                if product_name in self.available_stock:
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        if 'cart' in self.users[user_id]:
            del self.users[user_id]['cart']

        del self.users[user_id]
        return True

    def add_or_update_product(self, name, price, stock):
        if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        if name in self.products:
            self.products[name]['price'] = price
            self.products[name]['total_stock'] += stock
            self.available_stock[name] += stock
        else:
            self.products[name] = {
                'price': price,
                'total_stock': stock
            }
            self.available_stock[name] = stock

        return True

    def delete_product(self, name):
        if name not in self.products:
            return False

        if name in self.available_stock:
            del self.available_stock[name]

        del self.products[name]
        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products or quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
        return True

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        user_cart = self.users[user_id].get('cart', {})
        if product_name not in user_cart or user_cart[product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        if user_cart[product_name] == quantity:
            del user_cart[product_name]
        else:
            user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                    del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
                else:
                    self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if product_name in self.available_stock:
            self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity

        return True

    def checkout(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return False, None

        for product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return False, None

        total_price = 0.0
        for product_name, quantity in self.users[user_id]['cart'].items():
            total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

        original_cart = self.users[user_id]['cart'].copy()
        original_reservations = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()
        original_available_stock = {}
        for product_name in original_reservations:
            original_available_stock[product_name] = self.available_stock.get(product_name, 0)

        original_next_order_id = self.next_order_id

        try:
            order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"
            self.orders[order_id] = {
                'status': 'pending',
                'products': self.users[user_id]['cart'].copy(),
                'price_snapshot': {p: self.products[p]['price'] for p in self.users[user_id]['cart']},
                'total_price': total_price
            }

            self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
            if user_id in self.reservations:
                for product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
                del self.reservations[user_id]

            self.next_order_id += 1
            return True, order_id

        except Exception:
            if order_id in self.orders:
                del self.orders[order_id]

            self.users[user_id]['cart'] = original_cart
            if original_reservations:
                self.reservations[user_id] = original_reservations
                for product_name, quantity in original_reservations.items():
                    if product_name in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = original_available_stock.get(product_name, 0)

            self.next_order_id = original_next_order_id
            return False, None

    def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order['status']

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            if current_status in ['cancelled', 'delivered']:
                return False

            order['status'] = 'cancelled'

            for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    try:
                        if not product_name or order['price_snapshot'][product_name] < 0 or quantity < 0:
                            raise ValueError("Invalid product recreation parameters")
                        if not self.add_or_update_product(
                            name=product_name,
                            price=order['price_snapshot'][product_name],
                            stock=quantity
                        ):
                            raise ValueError("Failed to recreate product")
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"Error recreating product {product_name}: {str(e)}")
                        continue

            return True

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing'],
            'processing': ['completed'],
            'completed': ['shipped'],
            'shipped': ['delivered']
        }

        if (current_status in valid_transitions and
            new_status in valid_transitions[current_status] and
            current_status not in ['cancelled', 'delivered']):

            order['status'] = new_status
            return True

        return False

    def get_system_state(self, entity_type):
        if entity_type == 'users':
            return {user_id: user_data.copy() for user_id, user_data in self.users.items()}
        elif entity_type == 'products':
            return self.products.copy()
        elif entity_type == 'stock':
            return self.available_stock.copy()
        elif entity_type == 'carts':
            carts = {}
            for user_id, user_data in self.users.items():
                carts[user_id] = user_data.get('cart', {}).copy()
            return carts
        elif entity_type == 'orders':
            return self.orders.copy()
        else:
            return {}

def main():
    # Demonstration of the e-shop system
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create users
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    system.add_or_update_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    system.add_or_update_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)
    system.add_or_update_product("Tablet", 399.99, 8)

    # Add products to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 1)
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 2)
    system.add_to_cart("user2", "Tablet", 1)

    # Checkout
    success, order_id1 = system.checkout("user1")
    if success:
        print(f"Order created: {order_id1}")

    success, order_id2 = system.checkout("user2")
    if success:
        print(f"Order created: {order_id2}")

    # Update order status
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "processing")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "completed")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "shipped")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "delivered")

    # Cancel order
    system.update_order_status(order_id2, "cancelled")

    # Get system state
    print("\nCurrent system state:")
    print("Users:", system.get_system_state("users"))
    print("Products:", system.get_system_state("products"))
    print("Stock:", system.get_system_state("stock"))
    print("Carts:", system.get_system_state("carts"))
    print("Orders:", system.get_system_state("orders"))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer, Administrator

**Goal:**
- Customer: Purchase products, manage shopping cart, and track orders
- Administrator: Manage product inventory, monitor system state, and process orders

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create and delete user accounts
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts and reservations

2. **Product Management**
   - Add, update, and delete products
   - Track product stock levels (total and available)
   - Reserve and release product stock for cart operations

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Add/remove products to/from user carts
   - Adjust product stock based on cart modifications
   - Clear cart upon successful checkout

4. **Order Processing**
   - Create orders from user carts
   - Generate unique order identifiers
   - Track order status and support status transitions
   - Handle order cancellation and stock restoration
   - Maintain price snapshots for historical accuracy

5. **System Monitoring**
   - Provide current state of users, products, stock, carts, and orders

---

**Preconditions:**
1. System is initialized with empty user, product, and order repositories
2. For user-specific operations: User account exists
3. For product operations: Product exists (where applicable)
4. For cart operations: User has an active cart
5. For order operations: Order exists (where applicable)

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - New user account created with empty cart and details
   - User account and associated data (cart, reservations) removed

2. **Product Management**
   - New product added with specified price and stock
   - Existing product updated with new price/stock
   - Product and associated stock data removed

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Product added/removed from cart with corresponding stock adjustment
   - Cart reflects current product quantities

4. **Order Processing**
   - Order created with "pending" status and unique ID
   - Order status updated according to valid transitions
   - Cancelled orders restore product stock
   - System maintains consistent state after failed checkout attempts

5. **System Monitoring**
   - Current system state snapshot returned for requested entity type

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

**1. Create User Account**
1. Actor requests to create a new user account with a unique identifier
2. System validates the identifier is unique and non-empty
3. System creates a new user account with empty cart and details
4. System confirms successful creation

**2. Add/Update Product**
1. Actor provides product name, price, and stock quantity
2. System validates product details (non-empty name, non-negative price/stock)
3. System either:
   - Creates new product with specified details, or
   - Updates existing product with new price and increments stock
4. System confirms successful operation

**3. Add Product to Cart**
1. Actor specifies user ID, product name, and quantity
2. System validates:
   - User exists
   - Product exists and has sufficient stock
   - Quantity is positive
3. System:
   - Adds product to user's cart (or updates quantity)
   - Reserves product stock
   - Reduces available stock
4. System confirms successful addition

**4. Remove Product from Cart**
1. Actor specifies user ID, product name, and quantity to remove
2. System validates:
   - User exists
   - Product exists in user's cart
   - Quantity is positive and ≤ current cart quantity
3. System:
   - Removes specified quantity from cart (or deletes product entry)
   - Releases reserved stock
   - Increases available stock
4. System confirms successful removal

**5. Checkout**
1. Actor requests to checkout for a specific user
2. System validates:
   - User exists
   - User's cart is not empty
   - All cart products exist in system
3. System:
   - Calculates total order price
   - Creates order with "pending" status and unique ID
   - Captures price snapshot for all products
   - Clears user's cart
   - Releases all product reservations
4. System returns order ID to actor

**6. Update Order Status**
1. Actor specifies order ID and new status
2. System validates:
   - Order exists
   - Status transition is valid
3. System updates order status and:
   - For cancellation: restores product stock
   - For other transitions: progresses status according to workflow
4. System confirms successful update

**7. View System State**
1. Actor requests current state for a specific entity type (users, products, stock, carts, orders)
2. System returns a snapshot of the requested data

---

**Extensions:**

**1a. Create User Account - Invalid Identifier**
1. System detects identifier is empty or already exists
2. System rejects request and notifies actor

**2a. Add/Update Product - Invalid Details**
1. System detects invalid product details (empty name, negative price/stock)
2. System rejects request and notifies actor

**3a. Add to Cart - Validation Failure**
1. System detects any of the following:
   - User does not exist
   - Product does not exist
   - Insufficient stock
   - Invalid quantity
2. System rejects request and notifies actor

**4a. Remove from Cart - Validation Failure**
1. System detects any of the following:
   - User does not exist
   - Product not in cart
   - Invalid quantity
2. System rejects request and notifies actor

**5a. Checkout - Validation Failure**
1. System detects any of the following:
   - User does not exist
   - Cart is empty
   - Product in cart no longer exists
2. System rejects request and notifies actor

**5b. Checkout - System Failure**
1. During checkout, an unexpected error occurs
2. System:
   - Rolls back all changes (cart, reservations, stock, order)
   - Maintains system consistency
3. System notifies actor of failure

**6a. Update Order Status - Invalid Transition**
1. System detects:
   - Order does not exist
   - Invalid status transition
   - Attempt to modify cancelled/delivered order
2. System rejects request and notifies actor

**6b. Update Order Status - Product Recreation Failure**
1. During order cancellation, product recreation fails
2. System logs error and continues with remaining products
3. System confirms partial success to actor

**7a. View System State - Invalid Entity Type**
1. Actor requests state for unknown entity type
2. System returns empty dataset

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. **Stock Management**
   - System must maintain real-time stock availability
   - Stock reservations must be atomic with cart operations
   - Failed operations must restore stock to original state

2. **Order Integrity**
   - Order price snapshots must capture product prices at time of checkout
   - Order status transitions must follow predefined workflow:
     pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered
   - Order cancellation must restore product stock

3. **Data Consistency**
   - System must ensure consistency between carts, reservations, and stock
   - Failed operations must roll back all related changes

4. **Unique Identifiers**
   - Order IDs must be unique and follow format "SKXXXXX" (e.g., SK00001)

---

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.available_stock = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
            return False

        self.users[user_id] = {
            'cart': {},
            'details': {}
        }
        self.reservations[user_id] = {}
        return True

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
                if product_name in self.available_stock:
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        if 'cart' in self.users[user_id]:
            del self.users[user_id]['cart']

        del self.users[user_id]
        return True

    def add_or_update_product(self, name, price, stock):
        if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        if name in self.products:
            self.products[name]['price'] = price
            self.products[name]['total_stock'] += stock
            self.available_stock[name] += stock
        else:
            self.products[name] = {
                'price': price,
                'total_stock': stock
            }
            self.available_stock[name] = stock

        return True

    def delete_product(self, name):
        if name not in self.products:
            return False

        if name in self.available_stock:
            del self.available_stock[name]

        del self.products[name]
        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products or quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
        return True

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        user_cart = self.users[user_id].get('cart', {})
        if product_name not in user_cart or user_cart[product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        if user_cart[product_name] == quantity:
            del user_cart[product_name]
        else:
            user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                    del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
                else:
                    self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if product_name in self.available_stock:
            self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity

        return True

    def checkout(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return False, None

        for product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return False, None

        total_price = 0.0
        for product_name, quantity in self.users[user_id]['cart'].items():
            total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

        original_cart = self.users[user_id]['cart'].copy()
        original_reservations = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()
        original_available_stock = {}
        for product_name in original_reservations:
            original_available_stock[product_name] = self.available_stock.get(product_name, 0)

        original_next_order_id = self.next_order_id

        try:
            order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"
            self.orders[order_id] = {
                'status': 'pending',
                'products': self.users[user_id]['cart'].copy(),
                'price_snapshot': {p: self.products[p]['price'] for p in self.users[user_id]['cart']},
                'total_price': total_price
            }

            self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
            if user_id in self.reservations:
                for product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
                del self.reservations[user_id]

            self.next_order_id += 1
            return True, order_id

        except Exception:
            if order_id in self.orders:
                del self.orders[order_id]

            self.users[user_id]['cart'] = original_cart
            if original_reservations:
                self.reservations[user_id] = original_reservations
                for product_name, quantity in original_reservations.items():
                    if product_name in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = original_available_stock.get(product_name, 0)

            self.next_order_id = original_next_order_id
            return False, None

    def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order['status']

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            if current_status in ['cancelled', 'delivered']:
                return False

            order['status'] = 'cancelled'

            for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    try:
                        if not product_name or order['price_snapshot'][product_name] < 0 or quantity < 0:
                            raise ValueError("Invalid product recreation parameters")
                        if not self.add_or_update_product(
                            name=product_name,
                            price=order['price_snapshot'][product_name],
                            stock=quantity
                        ):
                            raise ValueError("Failed to recreate product")
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"Error recreating product {product_name}: {str(e)}")
                        continue

            return True

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing'],
            'processing': ['completed'],
            'completed': ['shipped'],
            'shipped': ['delivered']
        }

        if (current_status in valid_transitions and
            new_status in valid_transitions[current_status] and
            current_status not in ['cancelled', 'delivered']):

            order['status'] = new_status
            return True

        return False

    def get_system_state(self, entity_type):
        if entity_type == 'users':
            return {user_id: user_data.copy() for user_id, user_data in self.users.items()}
        elif entity_type == 'products':
            return self.products.copy()
        elif entity_type == 'stock':
            return self.available_stock.copy()
        elif entity_type == 'carts':
            carts = {}
            for user_id, user_data in self.users.items():
                carts[user_id] = user_data.get('cart', {}).copy()
            return carts
        elif entity_type == 'orders':
            return self.orders.copy()
        else:
            return {}

def main():
    # Demonstration of the e-shop system
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create users
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    system.add_or_update_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    system.add_or_update_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)
    system.add_or_update_product("Tablet", 399.99, 8)

    # Add products to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 1)
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 2)
    system.add_to_cart("user2", "Tablet", 1)

    # Checkout
    success, order_id1 = system.checkout("user1")
    if success:
        print(f"Order created: {order_id1}")

    success, order_id2 = system.checkout("user2")
    if success:
        print(f"Order created: {order_id2}")

    # Update order status
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "processing")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "completed")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "shipped")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "delivered")

    # Cancel order
    system.update_order_status(order_id2, "cancelled")

    # Get system state
    print("\nCurrent system state:")
    print("Users:", system.get_system_state("users"))
    print("Products:", system.get_system_state("products"))
    print("Stock:", system.get_system_state("stock"))
    print("Carts:", system.get_system_state("carts"))
    print("Orders:", system.get_system_state("orders"))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer, Administrator

**Goal:**
- Customer: Purchase products, manage shopping cart, and track orders
- Administrator: Manage product inventory, monitor system state, and process orders

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create and delete user accounts
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts and reservations

2. **Product Management**
   - Add, update, and delete products in the inventory
   - Track product stock levels and availability

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Add and remove products from user carts
   - Reserve stock for items in carts
   - Release reserved stock when items are removed or orders are cancelled

4. **Order Processing**
   - Process customer checkouts and generate orders
   - Update order status through predefined workflows
   - Handle order cancellation and stock restoration

5. **System Monitoring**
   - Provide current state of users, products, stock, carts, and orders

---

**Preconditions:**
1. System is initialized with empty user, product, and order databases
2. For user-specific actions, the user must exist in the system
3. For product-specific actions, the product must exist in the inventory (unless adding new)
4. For order-specific actions, the order must exist in the system

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - New user is created with empty cart and reservation records (on success)
   - User and all associated data are removed from the system (on success)

2. **Product Management**
   - Product is added/updated with correct price and stock levels (on success)
   - Product is removed from inventory and all associated stock records (on success)

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Product is added to user's cart with reserved stock (on success)
   - Product is removed from user's cart with stock released (on success)

4. **Order Processing**
   - Order is created with "pending" status and stock is deducted (on success)
   - Order status is updated according to valid transitions (on success)
   - Cancelled order has stock restored to inventory (on success)

5. **System Monitoring**
   - Current system state is returned for the requested entity type

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **Create User**
   1. Actor requests to create a new user with a unique ID
   2. System validates the user ID is unique and not empty
   3. System creates a new user account with empty cart and reservation records
   4. System confirms successful user creation

2. **Add/Update Product**
   1. Actor provides product name, price, and stock quantity
   2. System validates product details (name not empty, price and stock ≥ 0)
   3. System updates existing product or creates new product with provided details
   4. System updates available stock levels
   5. System confirms successful product addition/update

3. **Add Product to Cart**
   1. Actor specifies user ID, product name, and quantity
   2. System validates user exists, product exists, and sufficient stock is available
   3. System adds product to user's cart and reserves the stock
   4. System confirms successful addition

4. **Checkout**
   1. Actor requests to checkout for a specific user
   2. System validates user exists and cart is not empty
   3. System calculates total price based on current product prices
   4. System creates a new order with "pending" status
   5. System clears user's cart and releases reservations
   6. System returns order ID to actor

5. **Update Order Status**
   1. Actor specifies order ID and new status
   2. System validates order exists and status transition is valid
   3. System updates order status
   4. If status is "cancelled", system restores stock to inventory
   5. System confirms successful status update

6. **View System State**
   1. Actor requests current state for a specific entity type (users, products, stock, carts, orders)
   2. System returns a snapshot of the requested data

---

**Extensions:**

1. **Create User**
   - *1a. User ID is invalid or already exists*
     - System rejects the request and notifies actor

2. **Add/Update Product**
   - *1a. Product details are invalid (empty name, negative price/stock)*
     - System rejects the request and notifies actor

3. **Add Product to Cart**
   - *1a. User does not exist*
     - System rejects the request and notifies actor
   - *1b. Product does not exist or insufficient stock*
     - System rejects the request and notifies actor
   - *1c. Quantity is invalid (≤ 0)*
     - System rejects the request and notifies actor

4. **Checkout**
   - *1a. User does not exist or cart is empty*
     - System rejects the request and notifies actor
   - *1b. Product in cart no longer exists*
     - System rejects the request and notifies actor
   - *1c. System error during order creation*
     - System rolls back all changes and notifies actor

5. **Update Order Status**
   - *1a. Order does not exist*
     - System rejects the request and notifies actor
   - *1b. Invalid status transition*
     - System rejects the request and notifies actor
   - *1c. Attempt to cancel already cancelled/delivered order*
     - System rejects the request and notifies actor
   - *1d. Error during stock restoration for cancelled order*
     - System logs error and continues processing remaining products

6. **Remove from Cart**
   - *1a. User does not exist*
     - System rejects the request and notifies actor
   - *1b. Product does not exist in cart or quantity exceeds cart quantity*
     - System rejects the request and notifies actor
   - *1c. Quantity is invalid (≤ 0)*
     - System rejects the request and notifies actor

7. **Delete User**
   - *1a. User does not exist*
     - System rejects the request and notifies actor
   - *1b. User has active reservations*
     - System releases all reserved stock before deleting the user

8. **Delete Product**
   - *1a. Product does not exist*
     - System rejects the request and notifies actor

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. **Stock Management**
   - System must ensure stock levels are accurately tracked and updated during cart operations, checkouts, and order cancellations
   - Stock reservations must be released when items are removed from carts or orders are cancelled

2. **Order Processing**
   - System must support atomic checkout operations (all-or-nothing)
   - Order status transitions must follow predefined workflow: pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered
   - Order cancellation must restore stock to inventory

3. **Data Integrity**
   - System must maintain consistency between carts, reservations, and stock levels
   - System must handle errors during checkout by rolling back all changes

4. **System Monitoring**
   - System must provide accurate snapshots of current state for all entity types

---

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.available_stock = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
            return False

        self.users[user_id] = {
            'cart': {},
            'details': {}
        }
        self.reservations[user_id] = {}
        return True

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
                if product_name in self.available_stock:
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        if 'cart' in self.users[user_id]:
            del self.users[user_id]['cart']

        del self.users[user_id]
        return True

    def add_or_update_product(self, name, price, stock):
        if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        if name in self.products:
            self.products[name]['price'] = price
            self.products[name]['total_stock'] += stock
            self.available_stock[name] += stock
        else:
            self.products[name] = {
                'price': price,
                'total_stock': stock
            }
            self.available_stock[name] = stock

        return True

    def delete_product(self, name):
        if name not in self.products:
            return False

        if name in self.available_stock:
            del self.available_stock[name]

        del self.products[name]
        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products or quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
        return True

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        user_cart = self.users[user_id].get('cart', {})
        if product_name not in user_cart or user_cart[product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        if user_cart[product_name] == quantity:
            del user_cart[product_name]
        else:
            user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                    del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
                else:
                    self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if product_name in self.available_stock:
            self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity

        return True

    def checkout(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return False, None

        for product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return False, None

        total_price = 0.0
        for product_name, quantity in self.users[user_id]['cart'].items():
            total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

        original_cart = self.users[user_id]['cart'].copy()
        original_reservations = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()
        original_available_stock = {}
        for product_name in original_reservations:
            original_available_stock[product_name] = self.available_stock.get(product_name, 0)

        original_next_order_id = self.next_order_id

        try:
            order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"
            self.orders[order_id] = {
                'status': 'pending',
                'products': self.users[user_id]['cart'].copy(),
                'price_snapshot': {p: self.products[p]['price'] for p in self.users[user_id]['cart']},
                'total_price': total_price
            }

            self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
            if user_id in self.reservations:
                for product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
                del self.reservations[user_id]

            self.next_order_id += 1
            return True, order_id

        except Exception:
            if order_id in self.orders:
                del self.orders[order_id]

            self.users[user_id]['cart'] = original_cart
            if original_reservations:
                self.reservations[user_id] = original_reservations
                for product_name, quantity in original_reservations.items():
                    if product_name in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = original_available_stock.get(product_name, 0)

            self.next_order_id = original_next_order_id
            return False, None

    def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order['status']

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            if current_status in ['cancelled', 'delivered']:
                return False

            order['status'] = 'cancelled'

            for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    try:
                        if not product_name or order['price_snapshot'][product_name] < 0 or quantity < 0:
                            raise ValueError("Invalid product recreation parameters")
                        if not self.add_or_update_product(
                            name=product_name,
                            price=order['price_snapshot'][product_name],
                            stock=quantity
                        ):
                            raise ValueError("Failed to recreate product")
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"Error recreating product {product_name}: {str(e)}")
                        continue

            return True

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing'],
            'processing': ['completed'],
            'completed': ['shipped'],
            'shipped': ['delivered']
        }

        if (current_status in valid_transitions and
            new_status in valid_transitions[current_status] and
            current_status not in ['cancelled', 'delivered']):

            order['status'] = new_status
            return True

        return False

    def get_system_state(self, entity_type):
        if entity_type == 'users':
            return {user_id: user_data.copy() for user_id, user_data in self.users.items()}
        elif entity_type == 'products':
            return self.products.copy()
        elif entity_type == 'stock':
            return self.available_stock.copy()
        elif entity_type == 'carts':
            carts = {}
            for user_id, user_data in self.users.items():
                carts[user_id] = user_data.get('cart', {}).copy()
            return carts
        elif entity_type == 'orders':
            return self.orders.copy()
        else:
            return {}

def main():
    # Demonstration of the e-shop system
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create users
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    system.add_or_update_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    system.add_or_update_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)
    system.add_or_update_product("Tablet", 399.99, 8)

    # Add products to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 1)
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 2)
    system.add_to_cart("user2", "Tablet", 1)

    # Checkout
    success, order_id1 = system.checkout("user1")
    if success:
        print(f"Order created: {order_id1}")

    success, order_id2 = system.checkout("user2")
    if success:
        print(f"Order created: {order_id2}")

    # Update order status
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "processing")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "completed")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "shipped")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "delivered")

    # Cancel order
    system.update_order_status(order_id2, "cancelled")

    # Get system state
    print("\nCurrent system state:")
    print("Users:", system.get_system_state("users"))
    print("Products:", system.get_system_state("products"))
    print("Stock:", system.get_system_state("stock"))
    print("Carts:", system.get_system_state("carts"))
    print("Orders:", system.get_system_state("orders"))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer, Administrator

**Goal:**
- Customer: Purchase products, manage shopping cart, and track orders
- Administrator: Manage product inventory, monitor system state, and process orders

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create and delete user accounts
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts and reservations

2. **Product Management**
   - Add, update, and delete products
   - Track product stock levels (total and available)
   - Reserve and release product stock for cart operations

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Add/remove products to/from user carts
   - Adjust product stock based on cart modifications
   - Clear cart upon successful checkout

4. **Order Processing**
   - Create orders from user carts
   - Generate unique order identifiers
   - Track order status and support status transitions
   - Handle order cancellation and stock restoration
   - Maintain price snapshots for historical accuracy

5. **System Monitoring**
   - Provide current state of users, products, stock, carts, and orders

---

**Preconditions:**
1. System is initialized with empty user, product, and order repositories
2. For user-specific operations: User account exists
3. For product operations: Product exists (where applicable)
4. For cart operations: User has an active cart
5. For order operations: Order exists (where applicable)

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - New user account created with empty cart and details
   - User account and associated data (cart, reservations) removed

2. **Product Management**
   - New product added with specified price and stock
   - Existing product updated with new price/stock
   - Product and associated stock data removed

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Product added/removed from cart with corresponding stock adjustment
   - Cart reflects current product quantities

4. **Order Processing**
   - Order created with "pending" status and unique ID
   - Order status updated according to valid transitions
   - Cancelled orders restore product stock
   - System maintains consistent state after failed checkout attempts

5. **System Monitoring**
   - Current system state snapshot returned for requested entity type

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

**1. Create User Account**
1. Actor requests to create a new user account with a unique ID
2. System validates the user ID is unique and not empty
3. System creates a new user account with empty cart and details
4. System confirms successful creation

**2. Add/Update Product**
1. Actor provides product name, price, and stock quantity
2. System validates product details (non-empty name, non-negative price/stock)
3. System either:
   - Creates new product with specified details, or
   - Updates existing product with new price and increments stock
4. System confirms successful operation

**3. Add Product to Cart**
1. Actor specifies user ID, product name, and quantity
2. System validates:
   - User exists
   - Product exists and has sufficient stock
   - Quantity is positive
3. System:
   - Adds product to user's cart (or updates quantity)
   - Reserves product stock
   - Reduces available stock
4. System confirms successful addition

**4. Remove Product from Cart**
1. Actor specifies user ID, product name, and quantity
2. System validates:
   - User exists
   - Product exists in user's cart
   - Quantity is positive and ≤ current cart quantity
3. System:
   - Removes product from cart (or reduces quantity)
   - Releases reserved stock
   - Increases available stock
4. System confirms successful removal

**5. Checkout**
1. Actor requests to checkout for a user
2. System validates:
   - User exists
   - User's cart is not empty
   - All cart products exist in system
3. System:
   - Calculates total order price
   - Creates order with "pending" status and unique ID
   - Clears user's cart
   - Releases any remaining reservations
   - Increments order counter
4. System returns order ID to actor

**6. Update Order Status**
1. Actor specifies order ID and new status
2. System validates:
   - Order exists
   - Status transition is valid
3. System updates order status and:
   - For cancellations: restores product stock
   - For other transitions: advances status according to workflow
4. System confirms successful update

**7. View System State**
1. Actor requests current state for a specific entity type (users, products, stock, carts, orders)
2. System returns a snapshot of the requested data

---

**Extensions:**

**1a. Create User Account - Invalid ID**
1. System detects user ID is empty or already exists
2. System rejects the request

**2a. Add/Update Product - Invalid Details**
1. System detects invalid product details (empty name, negative price/stock)
2. System rejects the request

**3a. Add to Cart - Validation Failure**
1. System detects any of:
   - User does not exist
   - Product does not exist
   - Insufficient stock
   - Invalid quantity
2. System rejects the request

**4a. Remove from Cart - Validation Failure**
1. System detects any of:
   - User does not exist
   - Product not in cart
   - Invalid quantity
2. System rejects the request

**5a. Checkout - Validation Failure**
1. System detects any of:
   - User does not exist
   - Empty cart
   - Product no longer exists
2. System rejects the request

**5b. Checkout - System Failure**
1. During checkout, an unexpected error occurs
2. System:
   - Rolls back all changes (cart, reservations, stock, order counter)
   - Rejects the request

**6a. Update Order Status - Invalid Transition**
1. System detects:
   - Order does not exist, or
   - Invalid status transition (e.g., "delivered" to "processing")
2. System rejects the request

**6b. Update Order Status - Cancellation**
1. System detects order is being cancelled
2. System:
   - Updates status to "cancelled"
   - Restores product stock (recreating products if necessary)
3. System confirms successful cancellation

**7a. View System State - Invalid Entity Type**
1. System detects invalid entity type request
2. System returns empty data set

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. **Stock Management**
   - System must maintain real-time stock availability during cart operations
   - Stock reservations must be released upon cart modification or checkout

2. **Order Integrity**
   - System must ensure atomicity during checkout (all operations succeed or none)
   - Price snapshots must be preserved for historical accuracy

3. **Status Transitions**
   - Order status transitions must follow predefined workflow:
     `pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered`
   - Orders can be cancelled from any status except "cancelled" or "delivered"

4. **Data Consistency**
   - System must maintain consistent state across users, products, stock, and orders
   - Failed operations must not leave the system in an inconsistent state

---

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.available_stock = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
            return False

        self.users[user_id] = {
            'cart': {},
            'details': {}
        }
        self.reservations[user_id] = {}
        return True

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
                if product_name in self.available_stock:
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        if 'cart' in self.users[user_id]:
            del self.users[user_id]['cart']

        del self.users[user_id]
        return True

    def add_or_update_product(self, name, price, stock):
        if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        if name in self.products:
            self.products[name]['price'] = price
            self.products[name]['total_stock'] += stock
            self.available_stock[name] += stock
        else:
            self.products[name] = {
                'price': price,
                'total_stock': stock
            }
            self.available_stock[name] = stock

        return True

    def delete_product(self, name):
        if name not in self.products:
            return False

        if name in self.available_stock:
            del self.available_stock[name]

        del self.products[name]
        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products or quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
        return True

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        user_cart = self.users[user_id].get('cart', {})
        if product_name not in user_cart or user_cart[product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        if user_cart[product_name] == quantity:
            del user_cart[product_name]
        else:
            user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                    del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
                else:
                    self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if product_name in self.available_stock:
            self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity

        return True

    def checkout(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return False, None

        for product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return False, None

        total_price = 0.0
        for product_name, quantity in self.users[user_id]['cart'].items():
            total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

        original_cart = self.users[user_id]['cart'].copy()
        original_reservations = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()
        original_available_stock = {}
        for product_name in original_reservations:
            original_available_stock[product_name] = self.available_stock.get(product_name, 0)

        original_next_order_id = self.next_order_id

        try:
            order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"
            self.orders[order_id] = {
                'status': 'pending',
                'products': self.users[user_id]['cart'].copy(),
                'price_snapshot': {p: self.products[p]['price'] for p in self.users[user_id]['cart']},
                'total_price': total_price
            }

            self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
            if user_id in self.reservations:
                for product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
                del self.reservations[user_id]

            self.next_order_id += 1
            return True, order_id

        except Exception:
            if order_id in self.orders:
                del self.orders[order_id]

            self.users[user_id]['cart'] = original_cart
            if original_reservations:
                self.reservations[user_id] = original_reservations
                for product_name, quantity in original_reservations.items():
                    if product_name in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = original_available_stock.get(product_name, 0)

            self.next_order_id = original_next_order_id
            return False, None

    def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order['status']

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            if current_status in ['cancelled', 'delivered']:
                return False

            order['status'] = 'cancelled'

            for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    try:
                        if not product_name or order['price_snapshot'][product_name] < 0 or quantity < 0:
                            raise ValueError("Invalid product recreation parameters")
                        if not self.add_or_update_product(
                            name=product_name,
                            price=order['price_snapshot'][product_name],
                            stock=quantity
                        ):
                            raise ValueError("Failed to recreate product")
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"Error recreating product {product_name}: {str(e)}")
                        continue

            return True

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing'],
            'processing': ['completed'],
            'completed': ['shipped'],
            'shipped': ['delivered']
        }

        if (current_status in valid_transitions and
            new_status in valid_transitions[current_status] and
            current_status not in ['cancelled', 'delivered']):

            order['status'] = new_status
            return True

        return False

    def get_system_state(self, entity_type):
        if entity_type == 'users':
            return {user_id: user_data.copy() for user_id, user_data in self.users.items()}
        elif entity_type == 'products':
            return self.products.copy()
        elif entity_type == 'stock':
            return self.available_stock.copy()
        elif entity_type == 'carts':
            carts = {}
            for user_id, user_data in self.users.items():
                carts[user_id] = user_data.get('cart', {}).copy()
            return carts
        elif entity_type == 'orders':
            return self.orders.copy()
        else:
            return {}

def main():
    # Demonstration of the e-shop system
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create users
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    system.add_or_update_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    system.add_or_update_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)
    system.add_or_update_product("Tablet", 399.99, 8)

    # Add products to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 1)
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 2)
    system.add_to_cart("user2", "Tablet", 1)

    # Checkout
    success, order_id1 = system.checkout("user1")
    if success:
        print(f"Order created: {order_id1}")

    success, order_id2 = system.checkout("user2")
    if success:
        print(f"Order created: {order_id2}")

    # Update order status
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "processing")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "completed")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "shipped")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "delivered")

    # Cancel order
    system.update_order_status(order_id2, "cancelled")

    # Get system state
    print("\nCurrent system state:")
    print("Users:", system.get_system_state("users"))
    print("Products:", system.get_system_state("products"))
    print("Stock:", system.get_system_state("stock"))
    print("Carts:", system.get_system_state("carts"))
    print("Orders:", system.get_system_state("orders"))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

def test_main_success_scenario():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create user
    assert system.create_user("user1") is True

    # Add product
    assert system.add_or_update_product("product1", 10.0, 100) is True

    # Add to cart
    assert system.add_to_cart("user1", "product1", 5) is True

    # Checkout
    success, order_id = system.checkout("user1")
    assert success is True
    assert order_id is not None

    # Verify order status
    state = system.get_system_state("orders")
    assert state[order_id]["status"] == "pending"

def test_user_deletion_with_reservations():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create user and add product
    system.create_user("user2")
    system.add_or_update_product("product2", 20.0, 50)

    # Add to cart and checkout
    system.add_to_cart("user2", "product2", 10)
    system.checkout("user2")

    # Delete user
    assert system.delete_user("user2") is True

    # Verify user is deleted
    state = system.get_system_state("users")
    assert "user2" not in state

    # Verify stock is adjusted
    stock = system.get_system_state("stock")
    assert stock["product2"] == 40  # 50 - 10 (reserved) + 10 (returned on deletion)

def test_order_cancellation_with_stock_adjustment():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create user and add product
    system.create_user("user3")
    system.add_or_update_product("product3", 30.0, 30)

    # Add to cart and checkout
    system.add_to_cart("user3", "product3", 15)
    success, order_id = system.checkout("user3")
    assert success is True

    # Cancel order
    assert system.update_order_status(order_id, "cancelled") is True

    # Verify order status
    state = system.get_system_state("orders")
    assert state[order_id]["status"] == "cancelled"

    # Verify stock is adjusted
    stock = system.get_system_state("stock")
    assert stock["product3"] == 30  # 30 - 15 (reserved) + 15 (returned on cancellation)

- Missing error handling for failed user creation (program checks for existing user/empty ID but diagram assumes success)
- Missing error handling for failed product addition/update (program validates inputs but diagram assumes success)

===== user_feedback OUTPUT =====
The generated e-shop system does not satisfy the specification due to several critical inconsistencies in stock, reservations, and state handling.

Key issues:

1. **Incorrect checkout behavior**

* During checkout, reserved quantities are incorrectly returned back to available stock.
* Reserved stock should already be deducted at reservation time and must NOT be modified during checkout.
* This leads to incorrect stock values after checkout and breaks consistency across the system.

2. **Broken stock–reservation invariant**

* The system does not maintain a consistent relationship between:

  * available stock
  * user reservations
  * cart contents
* As a result, stock is often too high or too low after operations like checkout, cancel, or user deletion.

3. **Incorrect reservation cleanup**

* Reservations are deleted entirely after checkout or user deletion, but the system still assumes their existence in some places.
* This leads to KeyError exceptions and violates the requirement that operations on non-existing entities must be ignored.

4. **User deletion issues**

* When deleting a user, reservations are partially handled but may leave the system in an inconsistent state.
* Stock restoration does not correctly reflect reserved quantities.

5. **Order cancellation inconsistencies**

* Cancelling an order may incorrectly increase stock multiple times or in incorrect situations.
* The system does not properly ensure idempotency of cancellation.

6. **Non-robust access to reservations**

* The system assumes reservation entries always exist and accesses them directly.
* This leads to runtime errors (KeyError) instead of safely ignoring missing data.

Summary:
The system violates the core invariant of the specification: available stock must reflect total stock minus reserved quantities at all times. Reservation handling must be consistent and stock must only be modified at well-defined points (reservation and release), not during checkout.
===============================

===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.delete_user: 
def delete_user(self, user_id):
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Cancel all reservations for the user
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
            if product_name in self.available_stock:
                self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
        del self.reservations[user_id]

    # Remove user's cart
    if 'cart' in self.users[user_id]:
        del self.users[user_id]['cart']

    # Delete the user (orders remain unchanged)
    del self.users[user_id]

    return True

===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.add_to_cart: 
def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Validate product exists and quantity is valid
    if product_name not in self.products or quantity <= 0:
        return False

    # Check available stock
    if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
        return False

    # Update cart and reservations
    if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

    # Update reservations
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity
    else:
        self.reservations[user_id] = {product_name: quantity}

    # Deduct from available stock
    self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity

    return True

===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.remove_from_cart: 
def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Validate product exists in cart and quantity is valid
    user_cart = self.users[user_id].get('cart', {})
    if product_name not in user_cart or quantity <= 0 or quantity > user_cart[product_name]:
        return False

    # Remove quantity from cart
    if quantity == user_cart[product_name]:
        del user_cart[product_name]
    else:
        user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

    # Adjust available stock
    if product_name in self.available_stock:
        self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity

    # Update reservations
    if user_id in self.reservations and product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
        if quantity == self.reservations[user_id][product_name]:
            del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

    return True

===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.checkout: 
def checkout(self, user_id):
    # Step 1: Validate user exists and cart is not empty
    if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return False, None

    # Step 2: Validate all reserved products exist
    cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
    for product_name in cart:
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False, None

    # Step 3: Calculate total price
    total_price = 0.0
    price_snapshot = {}
    for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
        price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']
        total_price += price_snapshot[product_name] * quantity

    # Step 4: Generate order ID
    order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"

    # Step 5: Begin atomic transaction
    try:
        # Create order
        self.orders[order_id] = {
            'status': 'pending',
            'products': cart.copy(),
            'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
            'total_price': total_price
        }

        # Clear cart and reservations
        self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name in cart:
                if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                    del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
            if not self.reservations[user_id]:
                del self.reservations[user_id]

        # Increment order ID for next use
        self.next_order_id += 1

        return True, order_id

    except Exception:
        # Rollback all changes
        if order_id in self.orders:
            del self.orders[order_id]
        # Note: next_order_id is not decremented to maintain strict sequence
        return False, None
===============================


===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.update_order_status: 
def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
    # Validate order exists
    if order_id not in self.orders:
        return False

    order = self.orders[order_id]
    current_status = order['status']

    # Handle cancellation case
    if new_status == 'cancelled':
        if current_status in ['cancelled', 'delivered']:
            return False

        # Update order status
        order['status'] = 'cancelled'

        # Process each product in the order
        for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                # Increase available stock for existing product
                self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
            else:
                # Recreate product with order's price snapshot
                try:
                    price = order['price_snapshot'][product_name]
                    self.add_or_update_product(product_name, price, quantity)
                except Exception as e:
                    # Log error but continue with other products
                    print(f"Error recreating product {product_name}: {str(e)}")

        return True

    # Handle non-cancellation status transitions
    valid_transitions = {
        'pending': ['processing'],
        'processing': ['completed'],
        'completed': ['shipped'],
        'shipped': ['delivered']
    }

    # Check if transition is valid and order is not in terminal state
    if (current_status in valid_transitions and
        new_status in valid_transitions[current_status] and
        current_status not in ['cancelled', 'delivered']):

        order['status'] = new_status
        return True

    return False
