===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.available_stock = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
            return False

        self.users[user_id] = {
            'cart': {},
            'details': {}
        }
        self.reservations[user_id] = {}
        return True

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
                if product_name in self.available_stock:
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        if 'cart' in self.users[user_id]:
            del self.users[user_id]['cart']

        del self.users[user_id]
        return True

    def add_or_update_product(self, name, price, stock):
        if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        if name in self.products:
            self.products[name]['price'] = price
            self.products[name]['total_stock'] += stock
            self.available_stock[name] += stock
        else:
            self.products[name] = {
                'price': price,
                'total_stock': stock
            }
            self.available_stock[name] = stock

        return True

    def delete_product(self, name):
        if name not in self.products:
            return False

        if name in self.available_stock:
            del self.available_stock[name]

        del self.products[name]
        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products or quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
        return True

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        user_cart = self.users[user_id].get('cart', {})
        if product_name not in user_cart or user_cart[product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        if user_cart[product_name] == quantity:
            del user_cart[product_name]
        else:
            user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                    del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
                else:
                    self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if product_name in self.available_stock:
            self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity

        return True

    def checkout(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return False, None

        for product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return False, None

        total_price = 0.0
        for product_name, quantity in self.users[user_id]['cart'].items():
            total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

        original_cart = self.users[user_id]['cart'].copy()
        original_reservations = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()
        original_next_order_id = self.next_order_id

        try:
            order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"
            self.orders[order_id] = {
                'status': 'pending',
                'products': self.users[user_id]['cart'].copy(),
                'price_snapshot': {p: self.products[p]['price'] for p in self.users[user_id]['cart']},
                'total_price': total_price
            }

            self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
            if user_id in self.reservations:
                del self.reservations[user_id]

            self.next_order_id += 1
            return True, order_id

        except Exception:
            if order_id in self.orders:
                del self.orders[order_id]

            self.users[user_id]['cart'] = original_cart
            if original_reservations:
                self.reservations[user_id] = original_reservations

            self.next_order_id = original_next_order_id
            return False, None

    def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order['status']

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            if current_status in ['cancelled', 'delivered']:
                return False

            order['status'] = 'cancelled'

            for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    try:
                        if not product_name or order['price_snapshot'][product_name] < 0 or quantity < 0:
                            raise ValueError("Invalid product recreation parameters")
                        if not self.add_or_update_product(
                            name=product_name,
                            price=order['price_snapshot'][product_name],
                            stock=quantity
                        ):
                            raise ValueError("Failed to recreate product")
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"Error recreating product {product_name}: {str(e)}")
                        continue

            return True

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing'],
            'processing': ['completed'],
            'completed': ['shipped'],
            'shipped': ['delivered']
        }

        if (current_status in valid_transitions and
            new_status in valid_transitions[current_status] and
            current_status not in ['cancelled', 'delivered']):

            order['status'] = new_status
            return True

        return False

    def get_system_state(self, entity_type):
        if entity_type == 'users':
            return {user_id: user_data.copy() for user_id, user_data in self.users.items()}
        elif entity_type == 'products':
            return self.products.copy()
        elif entity_type == 'stock':
            return self.available_stock.copy()
        elif entity_type == 'carts':
            carts = {}
            for user_id, user_data in self.users.items():
                carts[user_id] = user_data.get('cart', {}).copy()
            return carts
        elif entity_type == 'orders':
            return self.orders.copy()
        else:
            return {}

def main():
    # Demonstration of the e-shop system
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create users
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    system.add_or_update_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    system.add_or_update_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)
    system.add_or_update_product("Tablet", 399.99, 8)

    # Add products to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 1)
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 2)
    system.add_to_cart("user2", "Tablet", 1)

    # Checkout
    success, order_id1 = system.checkout("user1")
    if success:
        print(f"Order created: {order_id1}")

    success, order_id2 = system.checkout("user2")
    if success:
        print(f"Order created: {order_id2}")

    # Update order status
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "processing")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "completed")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "shipped")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "delivered")

    # Cancel order
    system.update_order_status(order_id2, "cancelled")

    # Get system state
    print("\nCurrent system state:")
    print("Users:", system.get_system_state("users"))
    print("Products:", system.get_system_state("products"))
    print("Stock:", system.get_system_state("stock"))
    print("Carts:", system.get_system_state("carts"))
    print("Orders:", system.get_system_state("orders"))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here is the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer / Administrator

**Goal:**
- Customer: Purchase products, manage shopping cart, and track orders.
- Administrator: Manage product inventory, monitor system state, and update order statuses.

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create and delete user accounts.
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts and product reservations.

2. **Product Management**
   - Add, update, or remove products from the inventory.
   - Track total and available stock for each product.

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Allow users to add or remove products from their cart.
   - Reserve stock for products in carts to prevent overselling.

4. **Order Processing**
   - Process checkout to create orders.
   - Generate unique order IDs and track order status.
   - Support order status transitions (e.g., pending → processing → shipped → delivered).
   - Allow order cancellation and restocking of products.

5. **System State Monitoring**
   - Provide read-only access to current system state (users, products, stock, carts, orders).

---

**Preconditions:**
1. The e-shop system is initialized and operational.
2. For user-specific actions (e.g., add to cart, checkout), the user must exist in the system.
3. For product-specific actions (e.g., add to cart, update product), the product must exist in the inventory.
4. For order-specific actions (e.g., update status), the order must exist in the system.

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - *Create User:* A new user account is created with an empty cart and no reservations.
   - *Delete User:* The user account, cart, and reservations are removed. Reserved stock is released back to inventory.

2. **Product Management**
   - *Add/Update Product:* The product is added or updated in the inventory with the specified price and stock. Available stock is adjusted.
   - *Delete Product:* The product is removed from the inventory, and its stock is no longer tracked.

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - *Add to Cart:* The product is added to the user’s cart, and the available stock is reduced by the added quantity.
   - *Remove from Cart:* The product is removed or reduced in the user’s cart, and the available stock is increased by the removed quantity.

4. **Order Processing**
   - *Checkout:* An order is created with a unique ID, and the user’s cart is cleared. Product reservations for the user are released.
   - *Update Order Status:*
     - For valid transitions (e.g., pending → processing), the order status is updated.
     - For cancellation, the order status is set to "cancelled," and products are restocked.
   - *Order Cancellation:* Products in the cancelled order are restocked, and the order status is updated to "cancelled."

5. **System State Monitoring**
   - The system provides a snapshot of the requested entity type (users, products, stock, carts, or orders).

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **Create User**
   1. Actor requests to create a new user with a unique ID.
   2. System validates the user ID (non-empty and not already existing).
   3. System creates the user account with an empty cart and no reservations.
   4. System confirms successful creation.

2. **Delete User**
   1. Actor requests to delete an existing user.
   2. System validates the user exists.
   3. System releases all reserved stock for the user.
   4. System removes the user’s cart and account.
   5. System confirms successful deletion.

3. **Add or Update Product**
   1. Actor provides product details (name, price, stock).
   2. System validates the details (non-empty name, non-negative price and stock).
   3. System adds the product to inventory or updates existing product details.
   4. System adjusts available stock.
   5. System confirms successful addition/update.

4. **Delete Product**
   1. Actor requests to delete a product by name.
   2. System validates the product exists.
   3. System removes the product from inventory and available stock.
   4. System confirms successful deletion.

5. **Add Product to Cart**
   1. Actor specifies a user, product, and quantity to add to the cart.
   2. System validates the user, product, and quantity (positive and available stock).
   3. System adds the product to the user’s cart and reserves the stock.
   4. System confirms successful addition.

6. **Remove Product from Cart**
   1. Actor specifies a user, product, and quantity to remove from the cart.
   2. System validates the user, product, and quantity (positive and not exceeding cart quantity).
   3. System removes or reduces the product in the user’s cart and releases the reserved stock.
   4. System confirms successful removal.

7. **Checkout**
   1. Actor requests to checkout for a user.
   2. System validates the user exists and the cart is not empty.
   3. System calculates the total price of the cart.
   4. System creates an order with a unique ID, "pending" status, and a snapshot of product prices.
   5. System clears the user’s cart and releases reservations.
   6. System returns the order ID to the actor.

8. **Update Order Status**
   1. Actor specifies an order ID and a new status.
   2. System validates the order exists and the status transition is valid.
   3. System updates the order status.
   4. If the new status is "cancelled," system restocks the products in the order.
   5. System confirms successful status update.

9. **View System State**
   1. Actor requests the current state of a specific entity type (users, products, stock, carts, or orders).
   2. System retrieves and returns a snapshot of the requested entity type.

---

**Extensions:**

1. **Create User**
   - *User ID already exists or is invalid:*
     1. System rejects the request and notifies the actor.

2. **Delete User**
   - *User does not exist:*
     1. System rejects the request and notifies the actor.

3. **Add or Update Product**
   - *Invalid product details (e.g., negative price/stock):*
     1. System rejects the request and notifies the actor.

4. **Delete Product**
   - *Product does not exist:*
     1. System rejects the request and notifies the actor.

5. **Add Product to Cart**
   - *User, product, or quantity is invalid (e.g., insufficient stock):*
     1. System rejects the request and notifies the actor.

6. **Remove Product from Cart**
   - *User, product, or quantity is invalid (e.g., quantity exceeds cart):*
     1. System rejects the request and notifies the actor.

7. **Checkout**
   - *User does not exist or cart is empty:*
     1. System rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *Order creation fails (e.g., system error):*
     1. System rolls back changes (restores cart and reservations).
     2. System notifies the actor of failure.

8. **Update Order Status**
   - *Order does not exist or status transition is invalid:*
     1. System rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *Order cancellation fails (e.g., product recreation error):*
     1. System logs the error and continues processing other products.

9. **View System State**
   - *Invalid entity type requested:*
     1. System returns an empty response.

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. **Stock Reservation:**
   - The system must reserve stock for products added to a cart to prevent overselling.
   - Reserved stock must be released if the user removes the product from the cart or the cart is cleared (e.g., after checkout or user deletion).

2. **Order Consistency:**
   - The system must ensure atomicity during checkout (e.g., if order creation fails, the cart and reservations must be restored).

3. **Order Status Transitions:**
   - The system must enforce valid status transitions (e.g., "pending" → "processing" → "completed" → "shipped" → "delivered").
   - Cancelled or delivered orders cannot be updated further.

4. **Data Integrity:**
   - The system must maintain consistency between carts, reservations, and available stock at all times.

---

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.available_stock = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
            return False

        self.users[user_id] = {
            'cart': {},
            'details': {}
        }
        self.reservations[user_id] = {}
        return True

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
                if product_name in self.available_stock:
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        if 'cart' in self.users[user_id]:
            del self.users[user_id]['cart']

        del self.users[user_id]
        return True

    def add_or_update_product(self, name, price, stock):
        if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        if name in self.products:
            self.products[name]['price'] = price
            self.products[name]['total_stock'] += stock
            self.available_stock[name] += stock
        else:
            self.products[name] = {
                'price': price,
                'total_stock': stock
            }
            self.available_stock[name] = stock

        return True

    def delete_product(self, name):
        if name not in self.products:
            return False

        if name in self.available_stock:
            del self.available_stock[name]

        del self.products[name]
        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products or quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
        return True

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        user_cart = self.users[user_id].get('cart', {})
        if product_name not in user_cart or user_cart[product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        if user_cart[product_name] == quantity:
            del user_cart[product_name]
        else:
            user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                    del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
                else:
                    self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if product_name in self.available_stock:
            self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity

        return True

    def checkout(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return False, None

        for product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return False, None

        total_price = 0.0
        for product_name, quantity in self.users[user_id]['cart'].items():
            total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

        original_cart = self.users[user_id]['cart'].copy()
        original_reservations = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()
        original_next_order_id = self.next_order_id

        try:
            order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"
            self.orders[order_id] = {
                'status': 'pending',
                'products': self.users[user_id]['cart'].copy(),
                'price_snapshot': {p: self.products[p]['price'] for p in self.users[user_id]['cart']},
                'total_price': total_price
            }

            self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
            if user_id in self.reservations:
                del self.reservations[user_id]

            self.next_order_id += 1
            return True, order_id

        except Exception:
            if order_id in self.orders:
                del self.orders[order_id]

            self.users[user_id]['cart'] = original_cart
            if original_reservations:
                self.reservations[user_id] = original_reservations

            self.next_order_id = original_next_order_id
            return False, None

    def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order['status']

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            if current_status in ['cancelled', 'delivered']:
                return False

            order['status'] = 'cancelled'

            for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    try:
                        if not product_name or order['price_snapshot'][product_name] < 0 or quantity < 0:
                            raise ValueError("Invalid product recreation parameters")
                        if not self.add_or_update_product(
                            name=product_name,
                            price=order['price_snapshot'][product_name],
                            stock=quantity
                        ):
                            raise ValueError("Failed to recreate product")
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"Error recreating product {product_name}: {str(e)}")
                        continue

            return True

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing'],
            'processing': ['completed'],
            'completed': ['shipped'],
            'shipped': ['delivered']
        }

        if (current_status in valid_transitions and
            new_status in valid_transitions[current_status] and
            current_status not in ['cancelled', 'delivered']):

            order['status'] = new_status
            return True

        return False

    def get_system_state(self, entity_type):
        if entity_type == 'users':
            return {user_id: user_data.copy() for user_id, user_data in self.users.items()}
        elif entity_type == 'products':
            return self.products.copy()
        elif entity_type == 'stock':
            return self.available_stock.copy()
        elif entity_type == 'carts':
            carts = {}
            for user_id, user_data in self.users.items():
                carts[user_id] = user_data.get('cart', {}).copy()
            return carts
        elif entity_type == 'orders':
            return self.orders.copy()
        else:
            return {}

def main():
    # Demonstration of the e-shop system
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create users
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    system.add_or_update_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    system.add_or_update_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)
    system.add_or_update_product("Tablet", 399.99, 8)

    # Add products to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 1)
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 2)
    system.add_to_cart("user2", "Tablet", 1)

    # Checkout
    success, order_id1 = system.checkout("user1")
    if success:
        print(f"Order created: {order_id1}")

    success, order_id2 = system.checkout("user2")
    if success:
        print(f"Order created: {order_id2}")

    # Update order status
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "processing")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "completed")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "shipped")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "delivered")

    # Cancel order
    system.update_order_status(order_id2, "cancelled")

    # Get system state
    print("\nCurrent system state:")
    print("Users:", system.get_system_state("users"))
    print("Products:", system.get_system_state("products"))
    print("Stock:", system.get_system_state("stock"))
    print("Carts:", system.get_system_state("carts"))
    print("Orders:", system.get_system_state("orders"))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer, Administrator

**Goal:**
- Customer: Purchase products, manage shopping cart, and track orders
- Administrator: Manage product inventory, monitor system state, and process orders

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. Manage user accounts (creation, deletion)
2. Maintain product catalog with pricing and stock levels
3. Handle shopping cart operations (add/remove items)
4. Process orders and manage order lifecycle
5. Track product reservations during cart operations
6. Provide system state visibility for monitoring
7. Enforce stock availability constraints
8. Support order cancellation with stock restoration

---

**Preconditions:**
1. System is initialized with empty user base, product catalog, and order records
2. Unique identifiers exist for all entities (users, products, orders)
3. System maintains accurate stock levels for all products

---

**Postconditions:**
1. User accounts exist with associated carts and order history
2. Product catalog reflects current inventory with accurate pricing
3. Orders are recorded with complete status history
4. Stock levels are updated according to cart and order operations
5. System state can be retrieved for all entity types

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

**1. User Management**
1. Administrator requests user creation with unique ID
2. System creates user account with empty cart
3. Administrator requests user deletion
4. System removes user account and releases all reserved stock

**2. Product Management**
5. Administrator adds new product with name, price, and stock
6. System records product in catalog with available stock
7. Administrator updates existing product details
8. System modifies product information and adjusts stock
9. Administrator deletes product
10. System removes product from catalog and available stock

**3. Shopping Cart Operations**
11. Customer adds product to cart with specified quantity
12. System:
    - Validates product existence and stock availability
    - Reserves requested quantity
    - Updates cart contents
13. Customer removes product from cart
14. System:
    - Validates cart contents
    - Releases reserved stock
    - Updates cart contents

**4. Order Processing**
15. Customer requests checkout
16. System:
    - Validates cart contents
    - Calculates total price
    - Creates order with pending status
    - Clears customer cart
    - Generates unique order ID
17. Administrator updates order status
18. System:
    - Validates status transition
    - Updates order status
    - For cancellations: restores product stock

**5. System Monitoring**
19. Administrator requests system state for specific entity type
20. System returns current state of requested entities

---

**Extensions:**

**1a. User Creation Failure**
- User ID is invalid or already exists
- System rejects creation request

**2a. User Deletion Failure**
- User does not exist
- System rejects deletion request

**3a. Product Addition/Update Failure**
- Product details are invalid (negative price/stock)
- System rejects modification

**4a. Product Deletion Failure**
- Product does not exist
- System rejects deletion request

**5a. Add to Cart Failure**
- User, product, or quantity is invalid
- Product is out of stock
- System rejects addition

**6a. Remove from Cart Failure**
- Cart doesn't contain specified product/quantity
- System rejects removal

**7a. Checkout Failure**
- Cart is empty or contains invalid products
- System rejects checkout request

**8a. Order Status Update Failure**
- Order doesn't exist
- Invalid status transition requested
- System rejects status change

**9a. System State Request Failure**
- Invalid entity type requested
- System returns empty state

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. All operations must maintain data consistency between carts, stock, and orders
2. Order IDs must follow "SKXXXXX" format with sequential numbering
3. System must support atomic checkout operations with rollback capability
4. Stock reservations must be released when:
   - Items are removed from cart
   - User account is deleted
   - Order is cancelled
5. Price snapshots must be preserved for historical order accuracy

---

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.available_stock = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
            return False

        self.users[user_id] = {
            'cart': {},
            'details': {}
        }
        self.reservations[user_id] = {}
        return True

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
                if product_name in self.available_stock:
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        if 'cart' in self.users[user_id]:
            del self.users[user_id]['cart']

        del self.users[user_id]
        return True

    def add_or_update_product(self, name, price, stock):
        if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        if name in self.products:
            self.products[name]['price'] = price
            self.products[name]['total_stock'] += stock
            self.available_stock[name] += stock
        else:
            self.products[name] = {
                'price': price,
                'total_stock': stock
            }
            self.available_stock[name] = stock

        return True

    def delete_product(self, name):
        if name not in self.products:
            return False

        if name in self.available_stock:
            del self.available_stock[name]

        del self.products[name]
        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products or quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
        return True

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        user_cart = self.users[user_id].get('cart', {})
        if product_name not in user_cart or user_cart[product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        if user_cart[product_name] == quantity:
            del user_cart[product_name]
        else:
            user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                    del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
                else:
                    self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if product_name in self.available_stock:
            self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity

        return True

    def checkout(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return False, None

        for product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return False, None

        total_price = 0.0
        for product_name, quantity in self.users[user_id]['cart'].items():
            total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

        original_cart = self.users[user_id]['cart'].copy()
        original_reservations = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()
        original_next_order_id = self.next_order_id

        try:
            order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"
            self.orders[order_id] = {
                'status': 'pending',
                'products': self.users[user_id]['cart'].copy(),
                'price_snapshot': {p: self.products[p]['price'] for p in self.users[user_id]['cart']},
                'total_price': total_price
            }

            self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
            self.next_order_id += 1
            return True, order_id

        except Exception:
            if order_id in self.orders:
                del self.orders[order_id]

            self.users[user_id]['cart'] = original_cart
            if original_reservations:
                self.reservations[user_id] = original_reservations

            self.next_order_id = original_next_order_id
            return False, None

    def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order['status']

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            if current_status in ['cancelled', 'delivered']:
                return False

            order['status'] = 'cancelled'

            for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    try:
                        if not product_name or order['price_snapshot'][product_name] < 0 or quantity < 0:
                            raise ValueError("Invalid product recreation parameters")
                        if not self.add_or_update_product(
                            name=product_name,
                            price=order['price_snapshot'][product_name],
                            stock=quantity
                        ):
                            raise ValueError("Failed to recreate product")
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"Error recreating product {product_name}: {str(e)}")
                        continue

            return True

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing'],
            'processing': ['completed'],
            'completed': ['shipped'],
            'shipped': ['delivered']
        }

        if (current_status in valid_transitions and
            new_status in valid_transitions[current_status] and
            current_status not in ['cancelled', 'delivered']):

            order['status'] = new_status
            return True

        return False

    def get_system_state(self, entity_type):
        if entity_type == 'users':
            return {user_id: user_data.copy() for user_id, user_data in self.users.items()}
        elif entity_type == 'products':
            return self.products.copy()
        elif entity_type == 'stock':
            return self.available_stock.copy()
        elif entity_type == 'carts':
            carts = {}
            for user_id, user_data in self.users.items():
                carts[user_id] = user_data.get('cart', {}).copy()
            return carts
        elif entity_type == 'orders':
            return self.orders.copy()
        else:
            return {}

def main():
    # Demonstration of the e-shop system
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create users
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    system.add_or_update_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    system.add_or_update_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)
    system.add_or_update_product("Tablet", 399.99, 8)

    # Add products to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 1)
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 2)
    system.add_to_cart("user2", "Tablet", 1)

    # Checkout
    success, order_id1 = system.checkout("user1")
    if success:
        print(f"Order created: {order_id1}")

    success, order_id2 = system.checkout("user2")
    if success:
        print(f"Order created: {order_id2}")

    # Update order status
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "processing")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "completed")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "shipped")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "delivered")

    # Cancel order
    system.update_order_status(order_id2, "cancelled")

    # Get system state
    print("\nCurrent system state:")
    print("Users:", system.get_system_state("users"))
    print("Products:", system.get_system_state("products"))
    print("Stock:", system.get_system_state("stock"))
    print("Carts:", system.get_system_state("carts"))
    print("Orders:", system.get_system_state("orders"))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer / Administrator

**Goal:**
To enable users to browse products, manage shopping carts, place orders, and allow administrators to manage products and order statuses within an e-shop system.

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create and delete user accounts.
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts and reservations.

2. **Product Management**
   - Add, update, and delete products with pricing and stock information.
   - Track available stock and total stock for each product.

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Allow users to add and remove products from their carts.
   - Reserve stock for products in carts to prevent overselling.

4. **Order Processing**
   - Process user checkouts to create orders.
   - Generate unique order IDs and track order statuses.
   - Support order status transitions (e.g., pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered).
   - Allow order cancellation and restocking of products.

5. **Stock Management**
   - Update available stock when products are added to/removed from carts or orders.
   - Restock products when orders are cancelled.

6. **System State Monitoring**
   - Provide read-only access to system state (users, products, stock, carts, orders).

---

**Preconditions:**
1. The e-shop system is initialized and operational.
2. For user-specific actions (e.g., add to cart, checkout), the user must exist in the system.
3. For product-specific actions (e.g., add to cart), the product must exist and have sufficient stock.
4. For order-specific actions (e.g., update status), the order must exist in the system.

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - New users are created with empty carts and no reservations.
   - Deleted users are removed from the system, and their reserved stock is released.

2. **Product Management**
   - New products are added with specified price and stock.
   - Updated products reflect new price and/or stock values.
   - Deleted products are removed from the system, and their stock is cleared.

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Products added to a cart are reserved, reducing available stock.
   - Products removed from a cart release their reserved stock back to available stock.

4. **Order Processing**
   - Successful checkouts create orders with a unique ID, "pending" status, and a snapshot of product prices.
   - User carts are cleared upon successful checkout.
   - Order status updates follow valid transitions (e.g., pending → processing → completed).
   - Cancelled orders restock their products and release reservations.

5. **Stock Management**
   - Available stock is accurately updated based on cart and order operations.

6. **System State Monitoring**
   - System state queries return consistent and up-to-date data for users, products, stock, carts, and orders.

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **Create User**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the user ID is unique and not empty.
   - *System:* Creates a new user with an empty cart and no reservations.

2. **Add/Update Product**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the product name is not empty, and price/stock are non-negative.
   - *System:* Adds a new product or updates the price/stock of an existing product.
   - *System:* Updates available stock to reflect the new or updated product.

3. **Add Product to Cart**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:* Validates the user exists, the product exists, and sufficient stock is available.
   - *System:* Adds the product to the user's cart and reserves the stock.
   - *System:* Reduces available stock by the reserved quantity.

4. **Remove Product from Cart**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:* Validates the user and product exist, and the cart contains the specified quantity.
   - *System:* Removes the product from the cart and releases the reserved stock.
   - *System:* Increases available stock by the released quantity.

5. **Checkout**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:* Validates the user exists and the cart is not empty.
   - *System:* Calculates the total price of the cart.
   - *System:* Creates an order with a unique ID, "pending" status, and a price snapshot.
   - *System:* Clears the user's cart and increments the order ID counter.

6. **Update Order Status**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the order exists and the new status is valid.
   - *System:* Updates the order status (e.g., pending → processing).
   - *System:* If the order is cancelled, restocks the products and releases reservations.

7. **View System State**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Returns the requested system state (users, products, stock, carts, or orders).

---

**Extensions:**

1. **Create User**
   - *1a. User ID is invalid or already exists:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and returns an error.

2. **Add/Update Product**
   - *2a. Product name is empty or price/stock is negative:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and returns an error.

3. **Add Product to Cart**
   - *3a. User does not exist:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and returns an error.
   - *3b. Product does not exist or insufficient stock:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and returns an error.

4. **Remove Product from Cart**
   - *4a. User or product does not exist:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and returns an error.
   - *4b. Cart does not contain the specified quantity:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and returns an error.

5. **Checkout**
   - *5a. User does not exist or cart is empty:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and returns an error.
   - *5b. Product in cart no longer exists (e.g., deleted):*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and returns an error.
   - *5c. System failure during checkout:*
     - *System:* Rolls back the order creation, restores the cart, and returns an error.

6. **Update Order Status**
   - *6a. Order does not exist:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and returns an error.
   - *6b. Invalid status transition (e.g., delivered → processing):*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and returns an error.
   - *6c. Attempt to cancel a delivered or already cancelled order:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and returns an error.
   - *6d. Product recreation fails during cancellation:*
     - *System:* Logs the error and continues processing other products.

7. **View System State**
   - *7a. Invalid entity type requested:*
     - *System:* Returns an empty result.

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. **Stock Reservation:**
   - The system must reserve stock for products in carts to prevent overselling.
   - Reserved stock must be released if the product is removed from the cart or the order is cancelled.

2. **Order Consistency:**
   - The system must ensure atomicity during checkout (e.g., rollback on failure).
   - Order status transitions must follow a predefined sequence (e.g., pending → processing → completed).

3. **Data Integrity:**
   - The system must maintain consistent state for users, products, stock, carts, and orders.
   - Product price snapshots must be captured at checkout to ensure historical accuracy.

4. **Error Handling:**
   - The system must handle errors gracefully (e.g., rollback on checkout failure, log product recreation errors).

---

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.available_stock = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
            return False

        self.users[user_id] = {
            'cart': {},
            'details': {}
        }
        self.reservations[user_id] = {}
        return True

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            for product_name, quantity in self.reservations[user_id].items():
                if product_name in self.available_stock:
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        if 'cart' in self.users[user_id]:
            del self.users[user_id]['cart']

        del self.users[user_id]
        return True

    def add_or_update_product(self, name, price, stock):
        if not name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        if name in self.products:
            self.products[name]['price'] = price
            self.products[name]['total_stock'] += stock
            self.available_stock[name] += stock
        else:
            self.products[name] = {
                'price': price,
                'total_stock': stock
            }
            self.available_stock[name] = stock

        return True

    def delete_product(self, name):
        if name not in self.products:
            return False

        if name in self.available_stock:
            del self.available_stock[name]

        del self.products[name]
        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products or quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity

        self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
        return True

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        user_cart = self.users[user_id].get('cart', {})
        if product_name not in user_cart or user_cart[product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        if user_cart[product_name] == quantity:
            del user_cart[product_name]
        else:
            user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

        if user_id in self.reservations:
            if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                    del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
                else:
                    self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        if product_name in self.available_stock:
            self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity

        return True

    def checkout(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return False, None

        for product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return False, None

        total_price = 0.0
        for product_name, quantity in self.users[user_id]['cart'].items():
            total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

        original_cart = self.users[user_id]['cart'].copy()
        original_reservations = self.reservations.get(user_id, {}).copy()
        original_next_order_id = self.next_order_id

        try:
            order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"
            self.orders[order_id] = {
                'status': 'pending',
                'products': self.users[user_id]['cart'].copy(),
                'price_snapshot': {p: self.products[p]['price'] for p in self.users[user_id]['cart']},
                'total_price': total_price
            }

            self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
            if user_id in self.reservations:
                self.reservations[user_id].clear()
            self.next_order_id += 1
            return True, order_id

        except Exception:
            if order_id in self.orders:
                del self.orders[order_id]

            self.users[user_id]['cart'] = original_cart
            if original_reservations:
                self.reservations[user_id] = original_reservations

            self.next_order_id = original_next_order_id
            return False, None

    def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order['status']

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            if current_status in ['cancelled', 'delivered']:
                return False

            order['status'] = 'cancelled'

            for product_name, quantity in order['products'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    try:
                        if not product_name or order['price_snapshot'][product_name] < 0 or quantity < 0:
                            raise ValueError("Invalid product recreation parameters")
                        if not self.add_or_update_product(
                            name=product_name,
                            price=order['price_snapshot'][product_name],
                            stock=quantity
                        ):
                            raise ValueError("Failed to recreate product")
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"Error recreating product {product_name}: {str(e)}")
                        continue

            return True

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing'],
            'processing': ['completed'],
            'completed': ['shipped'],
            'shipped': ['delivered']
        }

        if (current_status in valid_transitions and
            new_status in valid_transitions[current_status] and
            current_status not in ['cancelled', 'delivered']):

            order['status'] = new_status
            return True

        return False

    def get_system_state(self, entity_type):
        if entity_type == 'users':
            return {user_id: user_data.copy() for user_id, user_data in self.users.items()}
        elif entity_type == 'products':
            return self.products.copy()
        elif entity_type == 'stock':
            return self.available_stock.copy()
        elif entity_type == 'carts':
            carts = {}
            for user_id, user_data in self.users.items():
                carts[user_id] = user_data.get('cart', {}).copy()
            return carts
        elif entity_type == 'orders':
            return self.orders.copy()
        else:
            return {}

def main():
    # Demonstration of the e-shop system
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create users
    system.create_user("user1")
    system.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    system.add_or_update_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    system.add_or_update_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)
    system.add_or_update_product("Tablet", 399.99, 8)

    # Add products to cart
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 1)
    system.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 2)
    system.add_to_cart("user2", "Tablet", 1)

    # Checkout
    success, order_id1 = system.checkout("user1")
    if success:
        print(f"Order created: {order_id1}")

    success, order_id2 = system.checkout("user2")
    if success:
        print(f"Order created: {order_id2}")

    # Update order status
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "processing")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "completed")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "shipped")
    system.update_order_status(order_id1, "delivered")

    # Cancel order
    system.update_order_status(order_id2, "cancelled")

    # Get system state
    print("\nCurrent system state:")
    print("Users:", system.get_system_state("users"))
    print("Products:", system.get_system_state("products"))
    print("Stock:", system.get_system_state("stock"))
    print("Carts:", system.get_system_state("carts"))
    print("Orders:", system.get_system_state("orders"))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== generate_integration_test OUTPUT =====
def test_main_success_scenario():
    system = EShopSystem()
    # Create user
    assert system.create_user("user1") is True
    # Add product
    assert system.add_or_update_product("product1", 10.0, 100) is True
    # Add to cart
    assert system.add_to_cart("user1", "product1", 5) is True
    # Checkout
    success, order_id = system.checkout("user1")
    assert success is True
    assert order_id == "SK00001"
    # Verify order status
    assert system.update_order_status(order_id, "processing") is True

def test_alternative_flow_cancel_order():
    system = EShopSystem()
    # Create user and add product
    system.create_user("user2")
    system.add_or_update_product("product2", 20.0, 50)
    # Add to cart and checkout
    system.add_to_cart("user2", "product2", 10)
    success, order_id = system.checkout("user2")
    # Cancel order
    assert system.update_order_status(order_id, "cancelled") is True
    # Verify stock was returned
    state = system.get_system_state("stock")
    assert state["product2"] == 50  # Original stock + cancelled quantity

def test_edge_case_delete_product_with_reservations():
    system = EShopSystem()
    # Create user and add product
    system.create_user("user3")
    system.add_or_update_product("product3", 15.0, 30)
    # Add to cart but don't checkout
    system.add_to_cart("user3", "product3", 5)
    # Delete product
    assert system.delete_product("product3") is True
    # Verify product is gone but reservation remains
    state = system.get_system_state("products")
    assert "product3" not in state
    # Verify user's cart still has the reservation
    cart_state = system.get_system_state("carts")
    assert "product3" in cart_state["user3"]

@startuml

:add product "Tablet" with price and stock;
:add 1 Laptop to user1's cart;
:add 2 Phones to user1's cart;
:add 1 Tablet to user2's cart;
if (user1's cart not empty) then (yes)
  if (all products exist) then (yes)
    :calculate total price;
    :generate order ID;
    :create order with pending status;
    :clear user1's cart and reservations;
    :increment next order ID;
    :confirm checkout success;
  else (no)
    :fail checkout;
  endif
else (no)
  :fail checkout;
endif
if (user2's cart not empty) then (yes)
  if (all products exist) then (yes)
    :calculate total price;
    :generate order ID;
    :create order with pending status;
    :clear user2's cart and reservations;
    :increment next order ID;
    :confirm checkout success;
  else (no)
    :fail checkout;
  endif
else (no)
  :fail checkout;
endif
:update order1 status to processing;
:update order1 status to completed;
:update order1 status to shipped;
:update order1 status to delivered;
if (order2 exists and not cancelled) then (yes)
  :update order2 status to cancelled;
  :adjust stock for cancelled products;
  :recreate missing products if needed;
else (no)
  :skip cancellation;
endif
:retrieve and display system state;
:show users, products, stock, carts, orders;
stop
@enduml

===== OUTPUT_review_activity_diagram OUTPUT =====
Here are the identified defects in the PlantUML activity diagram:

- Missing error handling for failed user/product creation (e.g., duplicate user or invalid product parameters) which exists in the program's validation logic
- Diagram shows sequential checkout for user1 and user2, but does not reflect the program's independent checkout calls with potential failure cases for each user
- Missing atomicity representation in checkout (program's try-except block for order creation rollback is not shown)
- Diagram shows "update order1 status" sequence without checking if order1 exists (program validates order existence before status update)
- Missing validation for order status transitions (program checks valid_transitions dictionary which is not reflected in the diagram)
- Diagram shows "recreate missing products if needed" as a single step, but program handles this per-product with error logging (overly abstracted)
- Missing representation of stock adjustments during order cancellation (program explicitly handles stock updates for existing products)
===============================
